M(1,\eta^{1}_{2}) &\otimes M(1,\eta^{1}_{2}) \simeq M(0,\eta^{2}_{0}) \oplus M(0,\eta^{2}_{2}) \oplus 2 M(0,\eta^{2}_{4}) \\
M(1,\eta^{1}_{2}) &\otimes M(1,\eta^{1}_{3}) \simeq M(0,\eta^{2}_{1}) \oplus M(0,\eta^{2}_{3}) \oplus 2 M(0,\eta^{2}_{5}) \\
M(1,\eta^{1}_{2}) &\otimes M(1,\eta^{1}_{4}) \simeq 2 M(0,\eta^{2}_{0}) \oplus M(0,\eta^{2}_{2}) \oplus M(0,\eta^{2}_{4}) \\
M(1,\eta^{1}_{2}) &\otimes M(1,\eta^{1}_{5}) \simeq 2 M(0,\eta^{2}_{1}) \oplus M(0,\eta^{2}_{3}) \oplus M(0,\eta^{2}_{5}) \\
\end{align*}

\subsubsection{$ M(1,\eta^{1}_{3})\otimes -$}
\begin{align*}
M(1,\eta^{1}_{3}) &\otimes M(1,\eta^{1}_{3}) \simeq 2 M(0,\eta^{2}_{0}) \oplus M(0,\eta^{2}_{2}) \oplus M(0,\eta^{2}_{4}) \\
M(1,\eta^{1}_{3}) &\otimes M(1,\eta^{1}_{4}) \simeq 2 M(0,\eta^{2}_{1}) \oplus M(0,\eta^{2}_{3}) \oplus M(0,\eta^{2}_{5}) \\
M(1,\eta^{1}_{3}) &\otimes M(1,\eta^{1}_{5}) \simeq M(0,\eta^{2}_{0}) \oplus 2 M(0,\eta^{2}_{2}) \oplus M(0,\eta^{2}_{4}) \\
\end{align*}

\subsubsection{$ M(1,\eta^{1}_{4})\otimes -$}
\begin{align*}
M(1,\eta^{1}_{4}) &\otimes M(1,\eta^{1}_{4}) \simeq M(0,\eta^{2}_{0}) \oplus 2 M(0,\eta^{2}_{2}) \oplus M(0,\eta^{2}_{4}) \\
M(1,\eta^{1}_{4}) &\otimes M(1,\eta^{1}_{5}) \simeq M(0,\eta^{2}_{1}) \oplus 2 M(0,\eta^{2}_{3}) \oplus M(0,\eta^{2}_{5}) \\
\end{align*}

\subsubsection{$ M(1,\eta^{1}_{5})\otimes -$}
\begin{align*}
M(1,\eta^{1}_{5}) &\otimes M(1,\eta^{1}_{5}) \simeq M(0,\eta^{2}_{0}) \oplus M(0,\eta^{2}_{2}) \oplus 2 M(0,\eta^{2}_{4}) \\
\end{align*}
